57: #   DROP TABLE/DATABASE/SCHEMA, TRUNCATE, docker system prune, kubectl delete,
58: #   chmod 777, fork bomb, mkfs, dd to raw disk.
59: """PreToolUse safety-guard — intercept destructive Bash commands.
60: 
61: Contract:
62: - stdin JSON: {"tool_name": "Bash", "tool_input": {"command": "..."}}
63: - exit 0: allow (proceed)
64: - exit 2: block (stderr message shown to Claude with reason + suggested alternative)
65: - fail-CLOSED on safety-gate corruption: stdin read failure / malformed JSON / evaluate() exception -> exit 2
66:   (closed by Ship 29.A.2 per Wave 29 Agent A REAL GPT-5.5 BRIDGE-MODE BLOCK + T3 R1 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.86 monotone-decline path)
67: - fail-open preserved on explicit allow paths: empty stdin / tool_name != "Bash" / empty command (no-command-to-check semantic)
68: 
69: Patterns matched (Careful Mode per ECC safety-guard SKILL.md):
70:   rm -rf / | rm -rf /* | rm -rf ~ | rm -rf * | rm -rf .
71:   sudo rm
72:   git push --force / -f
73:   git reset --hard
74:   git checkout .
75:   DROP TABLE / DATABASE / SCHEMA / INDEX (any case)
76:   TRUNCATE TABLE (any case)
77:   docker system prune
78:   kubectl delete
79:   chmod 777
80:   :(){ :|:& };: (fork bomb)
81:   mkfs.* (filesystem format)
82:   dd to /dev/[sh]d* (raw disk overwrite)
83:   --no-verify (always-block, overlaps with block_no_verify_guard)
84: 
85: Three-layer scan:
86:   1. Whole-command scan (fork-bomb class only — patterns containing `;`/`|` that
87:      per-sub splitter would fragment). Quote-stripped via _strip_quoted_regions.
88:   2. Per-sub scan: split on `;`/`&&`/`||`/newlines/`|`. For each sub:
89:      a. ALWAYS_BLOCK patterns fire even inside _DATA_PREFIXES (e.g. --no-verify
90:         inside `git commit` still blocks because the flag actually skips hooks).
91:         Exempted by _ALWAYS_BLOCK_EXEMPT_PREFIXES (echo/printf/grep/rg/cat/awk/
92:         sed/less/more — text tools where args are pure data).
93:      b. _DATA_PREFIXES (git commit / echo / printf / gh issue/pr/etc / heredoc) —
94:         skip pattern match because args are data, not subcommands.
95:      c. _DESTRUCTIVE patterns scanned against per-sub.
96: """
97: from __future__ import annotations
98: 
99: import json
100: import re
101: import sys
102: from pathlib import Path
103: 
104: _HOOKS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
105: if str(_HOOKS_DIR) not in sys.path:
106:     sys.path.insert(0, str(_HOOKS_DIR))
107: 
108: try:
109:     from utils import log_swallow
110: except ImportError:
111:     def log_swallow(exc: Exception, context: str) -> None:
112:         sys.stderr.write(f"[safety-guard:fallback] {context}: {exc}\n")
113: 
114: 
115: # Each pattern: (regex, human-readable description, suggested safer alternative)
116: # Keep patterns narrow to avoid false-positives (legitimate `git reset` non-hard, etc.)
117: _DESTRUCTIVE: tuple[tuple[re.Pattern[str], str, str], ...] = (
118:     # rm -rf followed by ONE of: standalone `/`, `~`, `*`, `/*`, or `.` (as whole argument)
119:     (re.compile(r"\brm\s+(-[a-zA-Z]*[rRfF][a-zA-Z]*\s+)+(/|~|\*|/\*|\.)(?=\s|$|;|\||&)"),
120:      "rm -rf against root/home/wildcard/glob/cwd",
121:      "specify an exact path; consider `git clean -fd` for tracked-file cleanup"),
122:     (re.compile(r"\bsudo\s+rm\b"),
123:      "sudo rm (elevated destructive delete)",
124:      "avoid sudo for file removal; drop privileges if possible"),
125:     (re.compile(r"\bgit\s+push\s+.*(--force\b|--force-with-lease\s+$|\s-f\b)"),
126:      "git push --force (rewrites remote history)",
127:      "use --force-with-lease with an explicit ref; coordinate with collaborators"),
128:     (re.compile(r"\bgit\s+reset\s+--hard\b"),
129:      "git reset --hard (discards uncommitted work)",
130:      "git stash first, or `git reset --keep` to preserve working tree"),
131:     (re.compile(r"\bgit\s+checkout\s+\.\s*$"),
132:      "git checkout . (discards ALL unstaged changes)",
133:      "git stash, or check `git status` first"),
134:     (re.compile(r"(?i)\bDROP\s+(TABLE|DATABASE|SCHEMA|INDEX)\b"),
135:      "SQL DROP (irreversible schema removal)",
136:      "BEGIN transaction first; take a backup; DROP in a migration file"),
137:     (re.compile(r"(?i)\bTRUNCATE\s+TABLE\b"),
138:      "SQL TRUNCATE (wipes all rows, skips triggers)",
139:      "DELETE with WHERE; TRUNCATE only in migrations"),
140:     (re.compile(r"\bdocker\s+system\s+prune\b"),
